<div id="post-batchaccountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### POST batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#post-batch-accounts-account-id-line-items "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

允许通过单个请求批量创建新的[广告项目](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。

**批量请求**

* 当前最大批量大小为 40。
* 所有参数都在请求正文中发送，并且必须将 `Content-Type` 设置为 `application/json`。
* 批量请求中的所有操作要么全部失败，要么全部成功，对于错误和成功的所有 API 响应都会保留初始请求中项目的顺序。

**批量响应**

批量 API 响应会返回一个有序的项目集合。除此之外，其结构与对应的单项目端点的响应完全相同。

**批量错误**

* 请求级错误（例如，超过最大批量大小）会在响应中的 `errors` 对象下显示。
* 项目级错误（例如，缺少必需的广告项目参数）会在响应中的 `operation_errors` 对象下显示。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                           | Description                                                                                    |
| :----------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| operation&#95;type  <br />*必需* | 正在对每个项目执行的操作类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `Create`, `Delete`, `Update` |
| params  <br />*必需*             | 一个包含所有广告项目参数的 JSON 对象。有关必需和可选广告项目参数的列表，请参见[此处](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。          |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`POST 'Content-Type: application/json' https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items`

```json
    [
      {
        "operation_type":"Create",
        "params":{
          "campaign_id":"8yn7m",
          "objective":"ENGAGEMENTS",
          "product_type":"PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "placements":"ALL_ON_TWITTER",
          "bid_amount_local_micro":3210000,
          "entity_status":"PAUSED"
        }
      }
    ]
```

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "name": null,
          "placements": [
            "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
          ],
          "start_time": null,
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
          "advertiser_domain": null,
          "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
          "primary_web_event_tag": null,
          "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "end_time": null,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "bid_strategy": "MAX",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": null,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
          "id": "9cqi0",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "android_app_store_identifier": null,
          "categories": [],
          "currency": "USD",
          "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
          "updated_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
          "creative_source": "MANUAL",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": [
        {
          "params": {
            "placements": [
              "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
            ],
            "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
            "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
            "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
            "entity_status": "PAUSED",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
            "campaign_id": "8yn7m"
          },
          "operation_type": "Create"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#put-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

更新与当前账户关联的指定 line item。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                                          | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :---------------------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                                  | 所使用账户的标识符。该标识符出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*必需*                            | 在请求中要操作的推广线项目的标识符。<br /><br />Type: 字符串<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| advertiser&#95;domain  <br />*可选*                           | 此广告主的网站域名，不包含协议部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当广告单元的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时，此字段为必需。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `x.com`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| advertiser&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*可选*                      | 用于推广 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告的账号对应的 Twitter 用户标识符。只有特定的客户端应用程序可以使用此参数。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `312226591`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| android&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*        | 针对所推广应用在 Google Play 商店中的标识符。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 投放目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符，即 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `com.twitter.android`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| audience&#95;expansion  <br />*可选*                          | 用于通过定向与已定向用户相似的用户来扩大广告活动的覆盖范围。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `BROAD`, `DEFINED`, `EXPANDED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| bid&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*              | 要与此 line item 关联的出价金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，5.50 美元表示为 5500000。<br /><br />**注意**：当 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `MAX` 或 `TARGET` 时为必需。<br /><br />**注意**：仅接受大于零的数值。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `140000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| bid&#95;strategy  <br />*可选*                                | 竞价机制。<br /><br />`AUTO` 会根据每日预算和广告活动投放周期自动优化出价。<br /><br />`MAX` 设置允许的最高出价，当目标设为 `REACH` 或 `FOLLOWERS` 时**不可用**。<br /><br />`TARGET` 尝试使每日平均出价与指定的 `bid_amount_local_micro` 的偏差不超过 20%，并且在目标设为 `REACH` 或 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：如果设置为 `AUTO`，则会忽略 `bid_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `AUTO`, `MAX`, `TARGET`                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| categories  <br />*可选*                                      | 与此广告主适用的 IAB 类别。请参阅 [GET iab&#95;categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时，此字段为必需。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`IAB3-1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| duration&#95;in&#95;days  <br />*可选*                        | 在该时间段内应用 `frequency_cap` 限制。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />可能的取值: `1`, `7`, `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选*                               | 广告项目的状态。<br /><br />类型：枚举<br /><br />可能的值：`ACTIVE`, `PAUSED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| end&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                    | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的时间点，届时该 line item 将停止投放。<br /><br />Type: 字符串<br /><br />Example: `2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| frequency&#95;cap  <br />*可选*                               | 向单个用户展示广告的最大次数。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于 `REACH`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS` 和 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告目标。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />示例: `5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| goal  <br />*可选*                                            | 此 line item 使用的优化设置。`APP_PURCHASES` 选项适用于 `APP_INSTALL`。`APP_CLICKS` 和 `APP_INSTALLS` 选项适用于 `APP_INSTALL` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS`，并且可能需要使用受支持的 [MACT 合作伙伴](https://business.x.com/en/help/campaign-setup/create-an-app-installs-or-app-engagement-campaign/mobile-app-conversion-tracking.html)。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于投放目标（objective）。<br /><br />类型：enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`APP_CLICKS`, `APP_INSTALLS`, `APP_PURCHASES`, `ENGAGEMENT`, `FOLLOWERS`, `LINK_CLICKS`, `MAX_REACH`, `PREROLL`, `PREROLL_STARTS`, `REACH_WITH_ENGAGEMENT`, `VIDEO_VIEW`, `VIEW_3S_100PCT`, `VIEW_6S`, `VIEW_15S`, `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` |
| ios&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*            | 用于推广的应用在 Apple App Store 标识符中的数字部分。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `333903271`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 名称  <br />*可选*                                              | 投放单元的名称。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `demo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| pay&#95;by  <br />*可选*                                      | 用于对此订单项计费的计费单位。此设置只能对使用 `APP_INSTALLS` 目标的订单项进行修改。<br /><br />**注意**：默认的 `pay_by` 会根据广告活动目标和订单项的出价单位自动设置。<br /><br />`APP_INSTALLS` 目标支持 `APP_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，`IMPRESSION` 为默认值。<br /><br />`LINK_CLICKS` 目标支持 `LINK_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，`IMPRESSION` 为默认值，但在将 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `TARGET` 时不支持。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 目标支持 `IMPRESSION` 取值。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `APP_CLICK`, `IMPRESSION`, `LINK_CLICK`                                                                                                                                                        |
| start&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                  | 此广告项目开始投放的时间，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给该广告单元的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于美元 (USD)，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `37500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给推广活动的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金来源关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。未提供时，推广活动将根据总预算和活动投放时长均匀消耗预算。仅当父级推广活动的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：该值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9cqi0?bid_amount_local_micro=140000`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "9cqi0",
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": null,
        "placements": [
          "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
        ],
        "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": null,
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": null,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "9cqi0",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "android_app_store_identifier": null,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
        "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
        "updated_at": "2022-06-03T23:51:36Z",
        "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

删除当前账户下指定的 line item。

**注意**：删除 line item 不可恢复，之后再尝试删除该资源将返回 HTTP 404。

**注意**：当一个 line item 被删除后，只有在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时，其子级 promoted&#95;tweets 才会在 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets 和 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets/:promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id 端点中返回。这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并不会被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。我们不会执行级联删除操作。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                           |
| :------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 要使用的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的 line item 的标识符。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `9f2ix`                                                                                                                 |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9f2ix`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": "Untitled",
        "placements": [],
        "start_time": null,
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 100000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": "2017-07-21T00:00:00Z",
        "duration_in_days": 1,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "9f2ix",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "frequency_cap": 5,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "created_at": "2017-07-14T00:01:50Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-09T07:41:08Z",
        "campaign_id": "90r8n",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "9f2ix",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="line-item-curated-categories">
  ### 广告项目精选分类
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

有关用法的更多详情，请参阅 [视频观看量前贴片目标指南](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categories">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-item-curated-categories "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的部分或全部 line item 精选类别的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :----------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*           | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                            |
| count  <br />*optional*                    | 指定每次请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                  |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                   | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                        |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*              | 按支持的属性以升序或降序排序。更多信息请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                      |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*         | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                   |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求有更低的速率限制，目前上限为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/abc1/line_item_curated_categories`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```josn
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "abc1"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "by5pw",
          "curated_category_id": "7op29tp2jzeo",
          "id": "1",
          "created_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "updated_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categoriesline_item_curated_category_id">

| line&#95;item&#95;curated&#95;category&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在请求中要操作的 line item curated category 的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`43853bhii885`                                                                                          |

<div id="media-creatives">
  ### 媒体素材

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#get-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部 media creative 的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*             | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                     |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*可选*            | 将响应限定为仅包含与指定 campaign 关联的 media creative。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8gdx6`                                                                                                                         |
| count  <br />*可选*                      | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                          |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                              |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*可选*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与指定 line item 关联的 media creative。最多可以提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8v7jo`                                                                                        |
| media&#95;creative&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的 media creative。最多可以提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `1bzq3`                                                                                                      |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*                | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `created_at-asc`                                                                                          |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?media_creative_ids=1bzq3`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "media_creative_ids": [
            "1bzq3"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
          "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
          "id": "1bzq3",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idmedia_creativesmedia_creative_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/media_creatives/:media_creative_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-media-creatives-media-creative-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定媒体创意（media creative）的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives/:media_creative_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 目标账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| media&#95;creative&#95;id  <br />*required* | 您在该请求中要操作的媒体创意（media creative）的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `43853bhii885`                                                                                                   |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*          | 在请求结果中包含已删除的记录。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives/1bzq3`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "media_creative_id": "1bzq3",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
        "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
        "id": "1bzq3",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#post-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

将一个 [account media](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 对象与指定的广告单元（line item）关联。

使用此端点在 Twitter Audience Platform 上推广流内广告（当 account media 的 `creative_type` 为 `PREROLL` 时）或图片广告（例如 `BANNER` 或 `INTERSTITIAL`）。

**注意**：要将媒体资产添加到 Account Media 资源，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/media&#95;library](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 端点。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />`required`            | 要使用的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                        |
| account&#95;media&#95;id  <br />`required`  | 你在请求中所操作的 account media 实体的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`10miy`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />`required`      | 你在请求中所操作的广告单元（line item）的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| landing&#95;url  <br />`sometimes required` | 将用户引导至的站点 URL。仅应与 TAP 图片（或“展示创意”）一起使用。如果与 preroll 资产一起使用，该值将被忽略。要将 URL 与 preroll 资产关联，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/preroll&#95;call&#95;to&#95;actions](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#post-accounts-account-id-preroll-call-to-actions) 端点。<br /><br />**注意**：当广告单元的目标设置为 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时，此字段为必需。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`https://blog.x.com/` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?line_item_id=8v7jo&account_media_id=10miy`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
        "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
        "id": "1bzq3",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

| media&#95;creative&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在此请求中要操作的媒体创意的标识符。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`1bzq3`                                                                                                                           |

```json
    {
```

### 推广账号

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下一个或多个推广线项所关联的部分或全部推广账户的详细信息。

使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 获取由响应中 `user_id` 标识的用户账户的用户数据。

如果指定的线项中没有任何一个被配置为包含推广账户，将返回 HTTP 400。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                             | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*               | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                 |
| count  <br />*optional*                        | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />类型：整数<br /><br />默认值：`200`  <br />最小值、最大值：`1`、`1000`                                                                                                                       |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                       | 指定一个游标以获取下一页结果。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8x7v00oow`                                                                                              |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*        | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与指定线项关联的推广账户。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`9bpb2`                                                                                                         |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的推广账户。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`19pl2`                                                                                                              |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                  | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`created_at-asc`                                                                                          |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*             | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：布尔值<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false`                                                                                                                    |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*     | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求适用更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型：布尔值<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?promoted_account_ids=19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_ids": [
            "19pl2"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "19pl2",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下与某个广告单元关联的特定账户引用记录。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在该请求中你要操作的推广账户引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*            | 在结果中包含已删除的记录。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                   |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个账户（`user_id`）与指定的广告单元关联。

如果指定的广告单元未配置为可与 Promoted Accounts（推广账号）关联，将返回 HTTP 400 `INCOMPATIBLE_LINE_ITEM` 错误。如果指定用户不符合推广资格，将返回 HTTP 400，并且不会推广任何用户。如果提供的用户已经在推广中，则会忽略该请求。

有关 Promoted Accounts 的更多信息，请参阅我们的[广告系列管理](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#advertiser-api)页面。

**注意**：无法更新（PUT）Promoted Accounts 实体。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| :------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                     |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中所操作的广告单元的引用。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `9bpb2`                                                                                                                                                                           |
| user&#95;id  <br />*required*          | 你在请求中所操作的用户的引用。使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 通过 screen name 获取用户 ID。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `756201191646691328` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?line_item_id=9bpb2&user_id=756201191646691328`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

将某个账号与指定的 line item 解除关联。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                                      | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :-------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*              | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）所必需的参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 指向与某个 line item 关联的 Promoted Account 实例的标识符。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`19pl2`                                                                                              |

**请求示例[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**响应示例[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-23T18:53:15Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promoted-tweets">
  ### 推广推文
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户下广告组（line items）关联的推文引用。

使用 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#get-accounts-account-id-tweets) 端点来获取 Tweet 对象。对每个 promoted&#95;tweets 对象使用其中的 `tweet_id` 值。

**注意**：当父级广告组被删除时，仅当在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时才会返回这些 promoted&#95;tweets。不过这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*             | 所用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                        |
| count  <br />*可选*                      | 指定每个单独请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />类型：int<br /><br />默认值：`200`  <br />最小值、最大值：`1`、`1000`                                                                                                                         |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8x7v00oow`                                                                                               |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*可选*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与特定广告组关联的推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`96uzp`                                                                                                               |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的推广推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`1efwlo`                                                                                                                  |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*                | 按支持的属性以升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`created_at-asc`                                                                                             |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：boolean<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型：boolean<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?promoted_tweet_ids=1efwlo`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_ids": [
            "1efwlo"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "96uzp",
          "id": "1efwlo",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账号下某个推广单元（line item）所关联的特定推文引用。

**注意**：当父级推广单元被删除时，只有在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 才会返回对应的 promoted&#95;tweets。这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并未被删除（在响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 用于投放的账号标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中所操作的推广推文引用。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `1efwlo`                                                                                                                         |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*          | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: boolean（布尔值）<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                     |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1efwlo`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1efwlo",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "96uzp",
        "id": "1efwlo",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个或多个推文与指定的广告单元关联。并非所有推文都适合推广，这取决于广告系列的目标。请参阅[基于目标的广告系列](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#objective-based-campaigns)了解更多信息。

当使用 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 产品类型时，将推文与 `line_item` 关联，除了标准的 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 展示位置外，还会在移动端时间线中增加展示位置。

**注意**：无法更新（PUT）推广推文实体。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                              |
| :------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有广告主 API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中要操作的广告单元的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                    |
| tweet&#95;ids  <br />*required*        | 以逗号分隔的标识符列表，对应特定推文。最多可提供 50 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `822333526255120384`                                                                                         |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?line_item_id=8v7jo&tweet_ids=822333526255120384`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "id": "1e8i2k",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "822333526255120384",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "tweet_ids": [
            822333526255120384
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "total_count": 1
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将某条推文与指定的广告单元（line item）解除关联。

**注意**：已删除的 promoted&#95;tweets 实体会在 ads.x.com UI 中显示为“Paused”。同样地，在 UI 中执行“暂停”操作也会将该推文从其广告单元中解除关联。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                          | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户相关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                                      |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 标识与某个广告单元（line item）关联的推广推文实例。该值来自 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets) 的响应项中的 `id` 字段，而不是该推文本身的 `tweet_id`。在资源路径中提供。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`1gp8a5` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1gp8a5`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9pl99",
        "id": "1gp8a5",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-08-17T17:02:21Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-18T06:43:48Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "844796297743757315",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1gp8a5",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promotable-users">
  ### 可推广用户
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_users">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部可推广用户的详细信息。

可推广用户类型为 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。该类型控制此账户允许推广的内容类型。广告主必须获得推广其他用户内容的权限，并联系 Twitter，将这些用户作为 `RETWEETS_ONLY` 类型的可推广用户添加到你的账户中。

在权限设置正确的前提下，你可以向推广产品相关的端点发起请求，直接引用你希望推广的推文的 Tweet ID。你可以使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广已发布的推文，也可以使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/scheduled-promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广其他 Twitter Ads 账户的计划推文。

你不必转发目标推文。当你使用此方式推广一条推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将不同于所提供的 Tweet ID。在后台，该推文会被以静默投放（nullcasted）推文的形式转发，然后再进行推广。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新的推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 用于投放的账户标识符。出现在资源的路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />类型：int<br /><br />默认值：`200`  <br />最小值、最大值：`1`、`1000` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8x7v00oow` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为所需的可推广用户。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`l310s` |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional* | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`created_at-asc` |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：boolean<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false` |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型：boolean<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users?promotable_user_ids=l310s`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_ids": [
            "l310s"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "l310s",
          "created_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "updated_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "deleted": false,
          "promotable_user_type": "FULL"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_userspromotable_user_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users-promotable-user-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定可推广用户。

可推广用户类型为 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。这将控制该账户被允许推广的内容类型。

广告主必须获得权限才能推广其他用户的内容。在权限设置正确的前提下，你可以向推广产品相关端点发起请求，直接引用你想要推广的推文的 Tweet ID。

你不必转发目标推文。当你使用这种方式推广一条推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将不同于你提供的 Tweet ID。在后台，该推文会以 nullcast 方式被转推为一条新的推文，然后再进行推广。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*optional* | 你在请求中要操作的可推广用户引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `l310s`                                                                                                                      |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                 |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users/l310s`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_id": "l310s",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "user_id": "2417045708",
        "id": "l310s",
        "created_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "deleted": false,
        "promotable_user_type": "RETWEETS_ONLY"
      }
    }
```

```json
          "content_category_ids": [
            "se"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendations">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

状态：*封闭 Beta*

检索与此广告账户关联的广告系列推荐。目前每个资金工具最多只能有 1 条推荐。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                         | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用广告账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    "request": {
      "params": {
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
      }
    },
    "total_count": 1,
    "data": [
      {
        "funding_instrument_id": "gpvzb",
        "id": "62ce8zza1q0w",
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
        "status": "PENDING",
        "message": "Recommendation for testing",
        "created_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z",
        "updated_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z"
      }
    ]
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendationsrecommendation_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations-recommendation-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

状态：*封闭测试版*

获取与此广告账户关联的特定广告系列推荐。

广告系列推荐包含一整套针对广告系列结构的建议更改，并以对象树的形式表示。该响应树旨在与 Batch API 端点配合使用，但也可以根据需要映射到单个更新端点（POST 对应 Create，PUT 对应 Update，DELETE 对应 Delete）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称 | 描述 |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用广告账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| recommendation&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在该请求中要操作的推荐 ID 引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`62ce8zza1q0w` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations/62ce8zza1q0w`
